Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 1

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使 Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元；公司同時將 2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%，反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險，油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變，但短線波動勢將加劇：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期，可見 AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加、期限溢酬及利率波動影響，因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

美期中選舉牽動市場表現，股債配置重品質、控風險
2026 年 11 月 04 日
一、 市場動態
1. 美期中選舉牽動市場情緒，亞股一度隨美股期貨下挫：美國 11 月 3 日舉行期中選舉
，根據 CNN 網站的結果顯示，截至台北時間 11/4 下午 3 點左右，民主黨於參議院
已取得 100 席中的 53 席的過半席次，於眾議院則在 435 席中取得了 225 席(218 席
過半)。市場重新評估國會控制權及川普政府未來政策推動能力，擔心民主黨國會影響
力擴大，可能對財政政策及科技企業監管形成新的制衡。S&P 500 期貨與 Nasdaq 100

期貨在開票期間一度下挫 1.38%及 2.35%，科技股明顯承受較大賣壓。惟隨後跌深反
彈，截至台北時間下午 3 時左右，S&P 500 期貨與 Nasdaq 100 期貨跌幅已收斂至
0.22%及 0.44%。亞洲市場方面，科技股為主及出口導向股市受到美股期貨拖累，台
灣加權指數 11/4 盤中最低下挫 3.38%，所幸隨跌幅收斂，終場下跌 0.83%；韓股同
日也由大跌近 4%由黑翻紅上漲 0.62%。11/3 未開盤的日股，在 11/4 開盤後出現補
跌，最低下挫近 4.8%，但最後跌幅收斂至下跌 1.42%。
2. 美國公債及美元同步反映政策預期調整，市場避險情緒升溫：美國公債及美元反映政
策預期調整，殖利率盤中先跌後漲：10 年期公債殖利率盤中先跌後漲，截至台北時間
下午 3 時來到 4.906%；同一時間，美元指數小跌約 0.37%。股、債、匯市的變動顯
示，投資人除了調整股票風險部位，也在評估未來美國財政刺激規模、政府債務供給
與利率前景。
二、 下跌原因
1. 民主黨取得國會控制權，市場重新定價財政與監管政策：本次選舉對金融市場的主要

影響，在於未來行政與立法部門的權力分配可能改變。共和黨先前同時掌握白宮與國
會，有利於推動稅制、財政及產業政策；民主黨在參眾兩院均取得過半席次後，部分

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元

政策將面臨更高的協商成本。財政方面，新增減稅及擴大支出的空間可能受到限制，
市場因而下修部分財政刺激預期；企業方面，國會監督權可能增加對大型科技企業、
AI 資料中心、反壟斷及資料隱私議題的調查壓力。惟國會控制權改變不代表既有政策
立即逆轉，也不代表所有監管措施均能完成立法。此次市場反應更可能是投資人重新
要求政策風險溢酬，而非企業獲利已發生實質下修。
2. AI 資本支出與投資回報疑慮尚未消退，放大科技股對政治事件的反應：此次選舉開票
期間，Nasdaq 期貨及與科技相關的亞股出現大幅波動，亦與近期科技股基本面及評

價爭議有關。10 月底以來美股因大型科技企業 AI 投資效益受到質疑而大幅修正，市
場關注焦點逐漸由 AI 基礎建設支出規模，轉向相關投資能否創造足夠的營運現金流與
股東報酬。以雲端服務及資料中心產業而言，企業雖可透過 AI 運算需求增加帶動營收
成長，但 GPU、伺服器、電力設備及資料中心建設需要龐大前期資金，若營收回收速
度不如預期，可能使自由現金流承壓並增加外部融資需求。近期部分科技企業籌資計
畫及財測不佳，已使投資人提高對財務品質的要求。當市場原先已對 AI 投資回報存有
疑慮，新的政治及監管不確定性便更容易放大評價修正幅度。
三、 市場看法
1. 半導體需求仍具分化，政治事件不等於 AI 產業成長趨勢反轉：科技股近期的股價修正
與 AI 終端需求惡化並非相同概念。AMD 近期公布優於市場預期的財報，並對資料中
心業務提出較強勁的成長展望，顯示 AI 運算需求仍具有一定韌性；相對而言，Intel
先前財測疲弱及現金流壓力，則反映不同半導體企業的產品競爭力、資本效率及終端
市場曝險存在明顯差異。因此，不宜將半導體股整體下跌解讀為 AI 需求全面降溫。從

產業鏈觀察，具備技術領先優勢、穩定客戶需求及良好現金流的企業，與高度依賴外
部融資、尚未形成穩定獲利模式的基礎建設業者，對資金成本及監管變化的承受能力
並不相同。未來市場可能更加重視個別企業的獲利品質，而非單純以 AI 相關營收成長
作為估值依據。
2. 政治事件可能延長科技股評價調整，但中期走勢仍取決於企業獲利：本次期中選舉引
發的期貨跌勢，反映市場對政策方向及國會控制權變化的即時反應，惟政治不確定性
通常須透過實際立法、企業投資計畫或經濟活動變化，才會形成較持久的基本面影響
。從企業獲利角度觀察，路透社引用 LSEG 資料指出，S&P 500 第三季企業獲利仍呈
現強勁成長，科技企業對整體獲利貢獻顯著，顯示目前尚無充分證據支持美國企業基
本面全面轉弱。不過，科技股先前已累積較高成長預期，市場對資本支出回報及監管
風險的容忍度降低，可能使股價即使在營收成長下仍承受評價壓力。短期而言，若選
舉結果逐漸明朗，且企業獲利預估未進一步下修，市場有機會逐步消化政治風險；反
之，若政策不確定性伴隨獲利預期下修，修正時間可能延長。

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元

不另行通知。
3. 歷史經驗顯示選後股市多正向，後續表現仍宜留意基本面變化：根據彭博統計，1950
年以來美國歷次期中選舉後 12 個月，S&P 500 平均報酬率約 15%，且 19 次全數
收正。其中最高為 1954 年的逾 30%，表現最弱的 1986 年亦小幅上漲。我們認為
，選前政策不確定性消除加上府會分治抑制財政與監管的轉向是為歷史上選後行情的
主要支撐，但相關數據所呈現的為過往經驗，未來股市表現能否延續先前情況仍應觀
察後續基本面變化。
4. 美國公債具避險價值，但利率下行能否持續仍取決於通膨與財政前景：在美國期中選

舉開票期間，美股期貨下跌伴隨美債殖利率回落，顯示公債仍能在股市波動期間提供
一定的風險分散效果。然而，本次殖利率下降可能同時包含避險買盤、財政刺激預期
下修及市場對經濟成長前景重新評估等因素，不宜直接解讀為聯準會即將轉向寬鬆。
尤其能源市場仍受到中東地緣政治及供應中斷風險影響，油價波動可能使通膨前景反
覆，限制長天期公債殖利率的下行空間。若未來財政擴張受到限制，可能對長端利率
形成部分支撐；但若政治僵局提高債務上限協商風險，亦可能使美債期限溢酬波動。
因此，債券配置仍須同時考量收益率、存續期間與通膨風險，不宜僅根據單日行情決
定配置方向。
5. 信用市場是判斷風險是否由股權評價擴散至金融條件的重要指標：目前科技股修正主
要反映評價與政策風險，但若 AI 企業持續增加資本支出及外部融資，市場對債務負擔
與償債能力的關注可能進一步升高。對大型投資等級科技企業而言，較穩定的營運現
金流及融資管道有助於吸收資本支出壓力；但對依賴單一客戶合約、設備抵押融資或
高槓桿經營的 AI 基礎建設業者，利率與信用利差上升可能更直接影響投資回報及再融

資能力。後續應追蹤投資等級債與高收益債 OAS、科技及雲端相關發行人利差、新債
發行需求及信用評等展望。若科技股下跌但整體信用利差維持穩定，市場風險可能仍
以股權評價調整為主；若信用利差同步持續擴大，則須提高對金融條件收緊及風險跨
市場擴散的警覺。

不另行通知。
四、 投資建議：
1. 科技股中期成長趨勢仍具支撐，惟短線宜審慎布局並重視個股分化：美國期中選舉帶
來的政策不確定性，短期可能使科技股評價承壓，惟大型雲端業者持續投入 AI 基礎建
設，半導體及相關設備需求仍具中期成長動能，目前尚不宜因美股期貨在開票期間的
下挫即全面轉向保守。我們仍建議維持美股核心配置，但短期避免過度集中於高評價
、高資本支出及高度依賴外部融資的企業。產業方面，資訊科技維持選擇性配置，優
先關注具備技術競爭優勢、穩定營運現金流及較高投資回報能見度的企業；對雲端基
礎建設及 AI 融資相關標的，則應提高財務結構與現金流審查標準。

2. 維持中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債配置，兼顧收益與風險分散：在美國政策風
險升溫及科技股評價調整期間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源
價格的不確定性，現階段不建議延長存續期間。建議以中天期美國公債及高信評投資
等級債作為主要配置方向，兼顧票息收益、利率敏感度與資產配置穩定性。信用債方
面，建議優先選擇營運現金流充足、槓桿適中及再融資需求可控的發行人，對 AI 相關
企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合約現金流與債務到期結構。

## Document 046: 20261118-台新投顧複委託報告-NVDA FY3Q27財報Call memo

請參考末頁之免責宣言說明                                                                             1

NVIDIA Corp. (NVDA-US)  
財報 FIRST TAKE — FY3Q27 NVDA 營收與財測全面優於預期，且上修 FY2028 營收成長預期

個股基本資料： 
NVIDIA Corp. (NVDA-US) 
日期 2026/11/17 
收盤價 $155.46 
股價表現 
三個月報酬(%) -30.9 
六個月報酬(%) -31.0 
一年報酬率(%) -16.6 
交易資料 
市值(十億) $3,762.9 
30 日平均成交量 180,644,268 
52 週高低 $148.80 - $243.37 
市場評等 
市場預估 12M 目標價 $263.8

資料來源：Bloomberg、FactSet 
 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
NVDA FY3Q27 營收、獲利及 Q4 財測都優於市場預期。財報公布後，股價
盤後一度上漲約 4%；董事會主席在法說會上表明不讓黃仁勳回任，漲幅一
度收斂。隨後暫代執行長 Kress 將 FY2028 營收成長指引由~70% 上修至
~75%，盤後漲幅再度擴大近 5%。一掃市場原先對公司可能會撤回長期展
望的擔憂；加上 Vera Rubin 創下公司史上最快放量，世代轉換十分順利。 
展望後市，市場普遍認為，AI 算力需求仍受供給限制，看好公司 FY2028 營
運能見度。CPU、網通與每座資料中心的產品含量提升，有助抵銷 ASIC 市
占提升的壓力；以 CY2027 本益比衡量，NVDA 評價仍低於費半。惟後續變
數在於 CEO 接班、美國新出口管制，以及相關調查與訴訟風險。整體而
言，基本面動能延續，惟治理與政策風險是評價折價的主因。 
公司財報重點數據： 
NVDA FY3Q27 營收 1,158 億美元，QoQ+20%、YoY+103%，優於市場預
期的 1,090 億美元。Non-GAAP 毛利率 74.9%，優於市場預期，主因產品組
合較佳。Non-GAAP EPS 2.68 美元，YoY+111%，優於市場預期的 2.47 美
元；自由現金流 437.8 億美元，較上季的 213.4 億美元明顯回升。本季以庫
藏股與股利合計返還股東 281 億美元（庫藏股約 220 億美元、現金股利約 
60 億美元），創歷史新高。董事會於 9 月核准增加 1,500 億美元庫藏股額
度，季末剩餘額度約 2,273 億美元，計畫於 FY2028 年底前執行完畢。 
業務部門表現： 
資料中心：營收 1,076 億美元，QoQ+21%、YoY+110%，優於市場預期。
其中 Vera Rubin 首季營收近 200 億美元，網通營收逾 210 億美元。 
⚫ 超大型雲端（Hyperscale ） 營收 600 億 美 元 ， QoQ+23% 、
YoY+71%，受惠 Vera Rubin 放量及 Grace Blackwell 需求延續。雲端
產業積壓訂單已由三個月前的 2 兆美元增至 2.5 兆美元。 
⚫ ACI&E（AI 雲端、工業、企業）營收 470 億美元，QoQ+18%、YoY
近 3 倍 。 季 增 動 能 來 自  Vera Rubin 出貨給主要新興 雲 端 業者
（Neocloud）及前沿模型實驗室自建資料中心。主權 AI 營收 YoY 逾 
3 倍；金融業地端營收過去 12 個月接近 50 億美元，年增逾 1 倍。 
⚫ 本季對中國出貨的授權 Hopper 資料中心產品營收為 24 億美元。 
邊緣運算：營收 82 億美元，QoQ+14%、YoY+42%，優於市場預期，主要
由企業端 Blackwell 工作站及實體 AI 邊緣運算需求帶動。 
複委託報告 November 18, 2026

### Page 2

2
公司展望：
(1).預估 FY4Q27 營收 1,300 億美元（±3%），優於市場預期；本季多一
週，且財測未納入任何中國資料中心運算營收。(2).預估 FY4Q27 毛利率
71%~72%，大致符合市場預期。公司表示 4Q 為毛利率低點，因需履行舊
價格訂單，新價格要到新年度才實施；FY2028 預計回升至 72%~73%，與
上季說法一致。(3). 預估 FY4Q27 GAAP / Non-GAAP 營業費用約 110 億 /
107 億 美 元 ， 反 映 多 出 一 週 及 交 接 相 關 成 本 ； FY2027 稅 率 維 持
16%~18%。(4). FY2028 全年營收成長由約 70%上修至約 75%（考量需求
成長、供應鏈支援增加，以及客戶資料中心整備與資金取得情況後的結
果）。(5). 預期 FY2028 CPU 營收、企業營收都將倍增以上。Vera CPU 新
增 CoreWeave、Meta、Nscale、OpenAI 等客戶。 (6). 產品藍圖不變：
Vera Rubin Ultra 預計明年推出，Feynman 預計 2028 年推出。DSX POD

模組化 AI 工廠預計明年初開始出貨美國及中歐客戶。(7). 供應鏈：本季新增
與 載 板 及 先 進 封 裝 業 者 的 產 能 合 作 ， 採 購 承 諾 增 至  3,900 億 美 元 。
Neocloud 相關承諾 320 億美元，公司表示將嚴格評估後續投資、承諾與保
證。(8). 中國：已停止出貨授權 Hopper 資料中心產品。美國商務部本週公
布新出口管制規則，公司仍在評估，但表示需求遠大於供給，預期營運仍維
持強勁。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
(1). FY2028 營收增速上修依據：公司表示，上修反映需求持續成長及新增
的供給；新出口管制仍在評估，但對 75% 有信心。(2). CEO 交接：董事會
主席開場即表明不會讓黃仁勳回任，並已委託獵才公司啟動遴選，內外部人
選都會考慮，且不會倉促決定。公司強調「營運一切如常」，客戶與供應商
全力支持，記憶體與先進封裝產能皆已確保。(3). Neocloud 融資與保證條
款： 公司表示，保證需待客戶完成融資、資料中心可啟用後才生效，這只

是說明現行做法，並非條款變更；未來支持客戶將更具選擇性，資本配置以
庫藏股為優先。(4). 2028 年放緩疑慮與產能分配：針對美國政局變化可能
帶來的政策風險，公司表示 Agentic AI 需求將延續到 2028 年，並持續與政
府溝通。產能分配依客戶資料中心整備度、資金到位及承接能力而定，所有
客戶價格一致。
公司簡介、競爭對手與公司自身價值：
NVIDIA 已轉型為 AI 基礎設施平台業者，護城河建立在 GPU、Vera CPU、
NVLink／Spectrum-X 網通、CPO 共同封裝光學與 CUDA 軟體生態系的
「極端共同設計」。公司表示，Vera Rubin 的每瓦 token 產出為 GB300 的
10 倍 ， 客 戶 導 入 時 間 只 有 Grace Blackwell 的 一 半 。 公 司 並 宣 布 收 購
Hugging Face，把影響力延伸至開源模型分發層。面對雲端業者加速自研
ASIC，NVDA 憑藉全端平台、供應鏈產能布局及生態系融資機制，維持 AI

算力市場的主導地位。

1 事件：10月外贸数据公布
2026年 10月，我国货物贸易（下同）进出口总值 4.87 万亿
元，增长 31.2%。其中，出口 2.79 万亿元，增长 28.7%；进口
2.08万亿元，增长 34.7%；贸易顺差 7127.76亿元。按美元计价，
10月我国进出口总值 7193.83亿美元，增长 37.9%。其中，出口
4123.3亿美元，增长 35.2%；进口 3070.53亿美元，增长 41.6%；
贸易顺差 1052.77亿美元。
2 事件解析：数据特征和变动原因
2.1 进出口维持高增
10 月进出口增速（美元计价，下同）维持高增，顺差仍在高
位。出口增速二季度以来维持强势，10 月高位小幅下降。从主要
出口市场表现来看，对美出口有所回落，增速仍在 30%以上，对
日本、东盟、欧盟出口增速均有所下降，对东盟的出口增速高达
40%以上。金砖四国方面，除巴西外均有所下降，但整体增速均
可观。从主要出口商品增速来看，除了通用设备、自动化数据处
理设备以及集成电路外均有所下滑，AI 相关产品出口仍维持高景

气。
进口增速同样有所加快，增速突破 40%。从进口数量来看，
主要进口商品增速除大豆、钢材以外均有所回升；从进口金额来
看，原油、煤、铜、二极管、集成电路有所回升，其中自动化数
据处理设备虽有所下降，但增速高达 261%。AI 相关产品的进口
需求较为强劲。

2.2 对主要国家出口增速高位回落
与 9月增速相比，今年 10月对主要国家出口增速虽有下滑，
但增速可观，对东盟出口增速超过 40%，对美出口也超过了 30%。

数据处理设备以及集成电路外均有所下滑，AI 相关产品出口仍维
持高景气。

2.4 顺差维持高位
10 月贸易顺差为 1052.77 亿美元，9 月为 1335.32 亿美元，
去年同期为 879.98亿美元。本月顺差维持在高位，但由于进口增
长更快，顺差高位回落。

3 事件影响：对经济和市场
3.1 AI 相关需求旺盛，进出口维持高增
10 月进出口增速维持高增。出口增速二季度以来维持强势，
10月高位小幅下降。从主要出口市场表现来看，对美出口有所回
落，增速仍在 30%以上，对日本、东盟、欧盟出口增速均有所下
降，对东盟的出口增速高达 40%以上。从主要出口商品增速来看，
除了通用设备、自动化数据处理设备以及集成电路外均有所下滑，
AI 相关产品出口仍维持高景气。进口增速同样有所加快，增速突
破 40%。从进口金额来看，原油、煤、铜、二极管、集成电路有
所回升，其中自动化数据处理设备虽有所下降，但增速高达 261%。
AI 相关产品的进口需求较为强劲。本月顺差维持在高位，但由于
进口增长更快，顺差高位回落。
4 事件预测：趋势判断
4.1 促进贸易平衡发展
我国外贸在此前关税扰动下表现韧性，我们认为主要还是中
国制造业在世界经济格局中的优势地位所决定的，作为全球制造

中心，这也是我国应对外部不确定性的优势，并且也得到了进一
步的加强巩固。我们认为外部环境的改善，中美关税继续暂停以

宏观数据
请务必阅读尾页重要声明 7
及全球 AI 产业链的发展，也推动了贸易的加速增长。政策方面，
根据 7 月政治局会议的安排，要拓展国际经贸互利合作空间，大
力发展服务贸易，促进贸易平衡发展；实施好更加积极的财政政
策和适度宽松的货币政策，充分发挥各项存量政策效能，及时谋
划出台务实管用的增量政策，加大逆周期调节力度，推动经济持
续向新向优向好发展，努力实现“十五五”良好开局。
5 风险提示：
地缘政治事件恶化，国际金融形势改变，中美政策超预期变
化。

### Page 1

00

SK 하이닉스 (000660/KS) 
여전히 강력하다 
3Q26 영업이익 83 조원으로 예상 상회 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 83조원 (+38% QoQ)으로 예상을 상회했다. 메모
리 공급 부족 심화, HBM4 반영 시작에 따른 메모리 가격 상승이 환율 하락을 상쇄했
기 때문이다. 출하 DRAM +10%, NAND -1%, 가격 DRAM +28%, NAND 
+24%, 영업이익률 DRAM 82%, NAND 72%, HBM 판가는 HBM4 출하가 시작되
며 37% 수준 상승한 것으로 추정된다. 업황은 여전히 예상을 상회하고 있다.

4Q26 분기 영업이익 100 조원 시대 진입 전망 
4Q26 영업이익은 105조원 (+27% QoQ)으로 분기 100조원 시대에 진입할 것으로 
전망한다. 메모리 가격 강세가 지속되는 가운데, 1c 출하 가속화, M15X 램프업 본격화 
등으로 출하량 상승이 기존 예상을 상회할 것이다. 또한 HBM4 의 Cross-over 시작 
및 수율 안정화로 HBM 가격, 수익성 제고 동반이 지속될 것이다. 출하는 DRAM 
+24%, NAND +4%, 가격은 DRAM +8%, NAND +7%를 예상한다.

2027 년 영업이익 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 상향조정 
SK 하이닉스의 2027년 영업이익을 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 12% 상향 조정한
다. 원/달러 환율 전망치를 기존 1,340 원에서 1,315 원으로 하향 조정했음에도, 공급 
부족 심화 속 2027년향 HBM 가격 인상 및 예상을 상회하는 수율, 1Q27 진행될 모바
일 DRAM 가격 인상 등을 반영했기 때문이다. LTA 가격 역시 개별 약정된 범위 내 
시장 상황을 반영할 수 있는 구조라는 점을 감안하면, 지속 상승할 것이라 전망한다.

높은 주주환원 의지 확인, 높아진 실적 가시성, 지나친 저평가 
투자의견 매수, 목표주가 4,000,000원을 유지한다. SK하이닉스는 LTA에 대해 기
존 5년에서 추가 연장에 대한 논의가 진행 중이며, 2029년까지 HBM, DRAM은 사실
상 완판, HBM 수요는 공급 가능 물량의 2 배를 상회, 2030 년까지 메모리 수요 우위 
국면 전망을 밝혔다. AI 인프라 투자의 강력한 명분과 메모리 수요의 구조성, 잠재적으
로 매우 높은 LTA 위반 비용을 감안하면 실적 가시성과 안정성 제고가 동반되는 국면
이다. 100 조원 수준의 주주환원은 2026 년 예상 FCF 의 66% 수준, 분기별 자사주 
매입, 소각 지속 계획을 통해 높은 주주환원 의지를 보여주고 있다. 실적 Duration 장
기화, 주주환원을 감안하면 12MF P/E 3.4X 수준의 현 주가는 현저한 저평가 국면이
다. 과도한 우려의 되돌림과 메모리 밸류에 대한 재논의 국면 진입을 전망한다.

2026-10-29

반도체. 한동희 / donghee.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-8826

## Document 225: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

기업분석 2026.10.30 www.daishin.com  
  Results Comment 
삼성전자 
(005930) 
 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 500,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
231,000 
반도체업종

◼ 3분기 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 
◼ 주주환원은 시점의 문제일 뿐. 강화 방향성은 여전히 유효. 
◼ 메모리반도체 이익의 폭증이 가격의 매력을 유발. 비중확대 전략 유효.

매력적인 가격, 변하지 않은 주주환원 정책의 방향성 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 메모리반도체 주도 폭발적 이익 상승이 가격 (주
가)의 매력을 지속 유발하고 있고, 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향성에도 변한 것
은 없다는 판단. 절대적으로 싼 주가를 비중 확대의 기회로 삼을 필요. 
1. 주주환원, 시점의 문제 
금번 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 대외 불확실성이 큰 만큼, 
시장은 자사주 매입/소각과 같은 즉각적 주가 부양 정책의 발표를 기대.  
당사는 삼성전자의 주주환원이 “시점의 문제”라 생각. 총 주주환원의 규모도, 
차기 (2027-2029년) 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향에도 변한 것은 없다는 판
단. 시장이 기대하는 자사주 매입/소각은 우선주 중심으로 시행될 가능성이 
높으며, 시행 시, 그 규모는 25조원 내외일 것 (DPS 12,567원)으로 전망. 
2. 메모리반도체, 더 올라갈 것 
보수적 환율 전망 (2027년 평균 환율 1,300원 적용)에도, 2027년 메모리반도
체 영업이익은 608조원에 달할 것. 이익 전망의 상향 여력 또한 유효. 
1) 최대 생산, 최대 판매 
삼성전자는 금일 4Q26 DRAM 판매량 가이던스로 전분기 대비 10% 중후반 
성장 (q-q +18%로 추정)을 제시. 일부 투자자들은 높아진 기저 부담으로 
2027년 판매 성장이 제한될 것을 우려. 당사는 높아진 기저에도 2027년 
DRAM 판매가 전년대비 20% 상승할 것으로 전망. 
- 극단적 수급 불균형 속, 삼성전자는 “최대 생산, 최대 판매” 정책을 시행 
중. 삼성전자만의 강력한 Execution이 20% 대 성장을 유지시킬 원동력. 
- DRAM 1c 수율의 조기 개선 속, 범용 DRAM 양산에 DRAM 1c Capa가 일
부 투입될 것으로 예상. Bit 성장을 높일 수 있는 변화가 될 것. 
2) 열려 있는 가격의 상방 
1Q27 모바일 DRAM에서 Surprise가 발생할 것이라는 의견을 유지 (전분기 
대비 30% 이상 상승). 장기계약이 체결된 CSP향으로도 가격의 추가 상승 여
력이 존재한다는 점을 상기할 필요. CSP의 경우, 당장의 비용 부담보다 AI 시
장 선점을 우선시. 장기계약으로 형성된 가격 Band의 천장 (Ceiling) 또한 시
장 우려 대비 낮지 않음을 감안할 필요. 2027년 범용 DRAM ASP는 전년대비 
40% 중후반까지 상승할 가능성 (현 전망: 전년대비 34% 상승).  
3) HBM, 제품 경쟁력과 점유율의 동반 성장  
최근 삼성전자향으로 HBM4/HBM4e 추가 공급 요청이 쇄도하고 있는 것으
로 추정. 일부 고객은 연내 추가 공급 시 2027년 계약 가격을 선제 지급할 
의사를 표출. 제품 경쟁력이 회복되었음을 보여주는 증거라 생각. 
제품 경쟁력의 회복과 최대 Capa의 이점 속, 2027년은 HBM 점유율 1위를 
확보하는 기념비적 해가 될 것으로 전망 (매출액 기준 점유율 38% 예상).

KOSPI 5937.66

 절대 (24.9) (6.2) (23.5)  
 상대 (13.1) (10.5) (47.4)  
 절대 (달러환산) (23.8) 1.5  (18.0)

3Q26 Review: “조삼모사” Again  
3Q26P 연결 실적은 매출액 6조 943억원(YoY -6.0%), 영업이익 4,520억원(YoY -46.2%, OPM 
7.4%)을 기록하며 컨센서스 대비 연결 매출액(7.5조원)과 영업이익(1.16조원) 모두 대폭 하회했다. 주
요 자회사가 양호한 실적을 기록했음에도 불구하고, 지상방산 실적이 매우 부진했다. 지상방산은 매출
액 1조 2,455억원(YoY -41.0%), 영업이익 217억원(YoY -96.2%, OPM 1.7%)을 기록했는데, 1)3Q 
생산 물량이 4Q 매출 인식되는 흐름 속, 고정비 부담이 컸었고, 2)낮은 수출 비중(3Q 48%, 2Q 58%)
과 국내외 사업의 부진한 믹스(국내 BEP 수준, 해외 OPM 11% 추정), 3)비우호적인 원/달러 환율, 4)
대전 사업장 사고 복구 및 기타 일회성 비용(300억원+160억원, 총 460억원) 등이 반영되었다.   
대전 사업장 정상화 시점과 사업 차질 영향은? 
대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 중 정상화될 예정이며, 보은과 여수 공장에서 생산 보완을 위한 조치가 진
행 중인 것으로 소통했다. 또한 대전 사업장은 국내 물량 위주이며, 수출 납기(천무, 천궁-II 추진체 
등)에는 지장이 없을 것이라고 언급했지만, 기타 사업장 램프업을 위한 인건비 증가 등, 수익성 악화는 
불가피할 것으로 예상된다.  
15%대의 매출 성장 가이던스는 달성이 가능할까? 
보수적으로 접근하는 것이 합리적이라는 판단이다. 당사는 ‘26E 지상방산 매출액을 일부 ‘27~28E로 
이연하면서 -6.1% 하향 조정했다. 사측은 폴란드 EC1 천무 인도 가이던스를 기존 40문에서 60문으
로 상향 조정하는 등 납품 속도가 예정대비 빨라진 것을 언급했다. 다만, 천무 EC1에서 미사일 물량 
비중이 높은 것으로 파악되기 때문에 생산 지연 가능성과 수출입 데이터 상 12월 출하 물량의 매출 인
식 이월 가능성을 반영했다. ‘27E는 폴란드 후속 사업과 루마니아 K9, 호주 레드백 등이 양산 페이즈
에 진입하며 성장세가 지속될 전망이다.    
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 1,480,000원 유지 
실적 쇼크와는 별개로, 미국 K9MH 시제, 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등 수주는 서프라이즈라고 판
단된다. 분기말 수주잔고는 35조원(수출 비중 74%, 스페인 미포함)으로 중장기 성장성에 대한 이견은 
없다. 불가피한 분기 실적 변동으로 동사의 미래 가치를 예단하는 것은 시기상조라는 판단이다.

## Document 270: Anthropic与SpaceX两大IPO前一个月，比特币均跌22%，流动性逻辑如何驱动后市行情？

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic与SpaceX两大IPO前一个月，比特币均跌22%，流动性逻辑如何驱动后市行情？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SBET ⾸席投资官将这⼀现象归因于 " 流动性事件迅速拉⾼资产间相关性 "—— 投资者抛售加
密资产以参与明星 IPO 。以此逻辑观察后市，多数券商要求投资者在获配新股后等待 15 ⾄
30 天⽅可卖出， SpaceX IPO 落地后，第 31 天 ETH 价格超越 IPO 当天价格，第 70 天 BTC 下
跌趋势逆转。
摘要：
⽐特币接连在两⼤重磅 IPO 前⼣上演⼏乎相同幅度的抛售，引发市场对资本争夺效应的⼴泛讨
论。这⼀规律性现象，正促使投资者重新审视加密市场与传统资本市场之间的流动性联动。
Anthropic IPO 前 30 天，⽐特币下跌 22.7% ； SpaceX 于 6 ⽉ 12 ⽇上市前⼀个⽉，⽐特币跌幅同
为 22.3% 。以太坊在同期亦分别录得 25.3% 与 28% 的跌幅，⾼度相似的⾛势令市场难以忽视其中
的潜在关联。
以太坊国库管理公司 SBET ⾸席投资官 Matthew Sheffield 将这⼀现象归因于" 流动性事件迅速拉
⾼资产间相关性 "，并指出 Anthropic 上市当⽇开盘 15 分钟内，科技股、原油、⻩⾦与加密货币即

同步遭到抛售。
⽬前随着 Anthropic 完成上市，市场焦点转向⽐特币与以太坊能否就此筑底，以及机构资⾦需求
能否重新回归。
⽐特币周五继续⼤跌 4.2% ，报 59318 美元，跌破 Nansen 研究分析师 Nikola Sondergaard 所划定
的 60000 ⾄ 62000 美元关键⽀撑区间。
IPO 前⼣抛售规律：巧合还是资本争夺
两次重量级 IPO 前后，加密资产均呈现近乎⼀致的抛售节奏，这⼀现象引发了关于 " 资本腾挪 " 的
推断 ——投资者从流动性较⾼的加密市场撤资，以参与⾥程碑式的 IPO 配售。
Matthew Sheffield 在接受采访时表⽰，这与新冠疫情初期市场集体奔向现⾦的逻辑如出⼀辙。
" ⽆论股票、债券还是⼤宗商品，⽆⼀例外，全部被变卖换取现⾦。价值储存类资产⻓期具备保
值属性，但短期⾛势仍由流动性主导。 " 他说。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
然⽽，这⼀判断⽬前尚⽆确凿证据⽀撑。加密货币同期还承受 ETF 资⾦流出、杠杆⻛险、宏观不
确定性以及市场普遍避险情绪的多重叠加压⼒，使得单⼀归因难以成⽴。
IPO 锁定期影响资⾦回流时点
即便 IPO 与加密市场之间存在资本联动效应，资⾦回流的时点也并⾮⽴竿⻅影，其中⼀个关键变
量是新股锁定期的安排。
Matthew Sheffield 指出，⼤多数券商要求投资者在获配新股后等待 15 ⾄ 30 天⽅可卖出，这意味
着财富效应的释放存在明显滞后。" 如果你在 SpaceX 上市当天做多以太坊，要等到第 31 天才会
实现盈利。 " 他说。
⽐特币国库机构 ASST ⾸席执⾏官兼董事⻓ Matt Cole 的观察印证了这⼀判断。
他表⽰， SpaceX IPO 落地后，⽐特币并未⽴即探明底部，真正的下跌趋势逆转发⽣在 IPO 后第

70 天。 " 我的判断是，⽐特币最有可能的情景是在当前区间震荡整理。 " 他说。
多重利空叠加，关键⽀撑位承压
⽐特币当前⾯临的抛压并不仅来⾃ IPO 资本争夺，更是多重负⾯因素的集中体现。
整个 11 ⽉，美元⾛强、机构持续抛售、加密杠杆盘爆仓等因素持续压制⽐特币价格。
下跌始于 10 ⽉末：市场对 AI 板块估值与资本开⽀的担忧引发科技股普跌，币安 63 亿美元被盗事
件、机构 ETF 需求⾛弱进⼀步加剧跌势。
Nansen 研究分析师 Nikola Sondergaard 将 60000 ⾄ 62000 美元区间定为⽐特币下⼀个关键考验
区间。周五，⽐特币周五继续⼤跌 4.2% ，报 59318 美元，跌破该区间下沿，价格或将进⼀步下
探 7 ⽉低点 57737.35 美元。
" 早春 " ⽽⾮盛夏：筑底尚需时⽇
在短期利空持续的背景下，部分市场⼈⼠认为当前更多是筑底过程的早期阶段，⽽⾮系统性⻛
险的引爆点。
Matt Cole 表⽰，近期系统故障、量⼦安全顾虑等短期不确定性放⼤了散⼾恐慌情绪，但债务扩

张、法币⾛弱等宏观因素仍将⽀撑⽐特币的中⻓期⾛势。
" 资本市场仍在正常运转，散⼾恐慌情绪⾼涨。综合来看，这更像是⼀次可以逢低布局包括⽐特
币在内的⻛险资产的机会，⽽⾮新⼀轮重⼤⾦融危机的开端。 " 他说。
他以季节更迭作⽐，描述当前市场状态： " ⽐特币的寒冬基本快要结束，但寒冬过后并不会直接
迎来盛夏，⽽是早春。经历过早春的⼈都知道，依旧会有寒潮、阴⾬，天⽓并不会⽴刻转好。 "
在他看来，现阶段更适合以波动视⻆看待⻛险资产配置，⽽不是全盘回避。

## Document 292: Capital Market Outlook (2026-11-16)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-11-16).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 November 16, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Inflation Sticky but Overheating Less Clear
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The disconnect between very strong recent growth with stubborn inflation above the Fed’s 2%
target on one hand and more moderate business surveys and labor-market indicators on the
other, has complicated the optimal Fed policy path and contributed to large swings in interest

rates. Indeed, the economy looks strong in several respects. Real GDP grew at a 3.4% annualized
pace in Q3, led by 3.8% real consumer spending growth, according to Bureau of Economic
Analysis (BEA), surging AI-related business investment, and a 16% annualized increase in real
federal government spending. As policy uncertainty eased from its tariff-related spike and growth
remained resilient, labor data have also improved. Job openings and quits rates have stabilized.

The October payrolls reports exceeded expectations. The unemployment rate, according to
Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS), slipped to 4.1% from 4.5% in November 2025.
Stronger hiring alongside higher-than-expected October consumer price index (CPI) inflation revived
expectations for further Fed tightening, however, causing renewed sharp interest rate increases.
Yet, as discussed below, the recent strong growth reacceleration followed a period of softer, tariff-

shock-related economic conditions, and some of its driving forces are likely to moderate. Moreover,
inflation still appears disproportionately related to energy, tariffs, freight costs and increasing
AI-related electronics inflation. Tariffs have already started to fade, though, while diesel prices have
potential to decline once geopolitical conflicts resolve. In this context, there is a risk that higher
interest rates would not only overly restrain demand but would also delay investment in housing,

power generation and other capacity expansion needed to keep inflation in check.
Here are a few details underpinning this view:
• The strong Q3 real GDP growth acceleration followed just a 1.6% annualized pace over the preceding
three quarters, with consumer spending also up at a moderate 2.1% average rate. At about 2.1%
and 2.5% year-over-year (YoY) growth, respectively, neither GDP nor consumption growth appear
exceptional, according to BEA. The Chicago Fed National Activity Index, which aggregates information

from 85 economic indicators seems to concur—following three years of subtrend readings, it has
oscillated between slightly above- and below-trend growth territory for most of the year.
• Business surveys also suggest ongoing expansion but not booming growth. The National Federation
of Independent Business Small Business Optimism Index remains below its July level even as job
openings, hiring intentions, and plans to raise worker pay spiked in October. The October Institute

for Supply Management (ISM) manufacturing survey has remained a bright spot, with production and
employment numbers particularly robust. While the overall index is associated with roughly 2.7% real
GDP growth, according to the ISM, its services counterpart has lagged, with below-average readings
across new orders, production, and employment in October. As a result, the weighted average of the
two surveys remains way below levels seen in prior expansions (Exhibit 1), in line with the moderate

growth dynamics pervading the Fed’s October Beige Book, for example.
• According to BLS, hiring was unquestionably strong in October. Payrolls gained 165,000. Hiring in
previous months was revised up. The three-month average jobs growth shifted to 137,000 from just
28,000 in July. Unemployment dropped to 4.1%. Still, part of the improvement represents catch-up
from the hiring lull observed after the tariff shock and related spike in policy uncertainty (Exhibit 2).

Labor turnover remains low, with the quits rate unsurprisingly depressed given the sharp uptrend
in the average duration of unemployment, which increased in October to a five-year high (also a
historically high level).
• Labor supply has also surprised to the upside as employment strengthened, precluding excessive wage
growth outside specific areas of technically skilled labor scarcity. After cratering in the year following
the tariff shock, the labor force participation rate (LFPR) has meaningfully rebounded since July,

helping bring nearly 1.6 million people back looking for work, according to BLS. Indeed, the supply of
labor is partly endogenous to economic conditions—it rises when jobs availability and hiring improve,
and vice versa. Although the prime-age female LFPR is near recent records, a rebound in participation
rates for males age 20 to 54 toward their 1995 to 2000 highs would allow meaningful additional
employment growth before wage-related inflation pressures become an issue.

In itself, renewed hiring following the tariff-related hiatus therefore need not be inflationary. Higher
employment alongside rising labor force participation and solid productivity growth should help
contain unit labor costs. It would also help expand the supply side to better match resilient demand,
helping keep inflation in check and prolonging the expansion.
Investment Implications
We remain overweight Equities,
favor U.S. stocks and see potential

opportunities in broader market
participation and cyclical sectors.
Treasury yields appear attractive for
income generation and diversification
reasons.

### Page 3

3 of 8 November 16, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
• Several supports to recent growth appear unsustainable. Base effects and midterm elections
results suggest a likely lessened growth impetus from AI investment. Faster inventory
accumulation, which significantly boosted Q3 real GDP, cannot continue indefinitely. Also, real
consumer spending barely inched higher in September following an August spike, according to
BEA. Indeed, consumer spending has increasingly outpaced income growth this year, sharply

eroding saving out of income from 6.2% in January to 3.8% in September, limiting the scope
for spending to keep outpacing income. Finally, while Q3 earnings have continued to positively
surprise across most sectors, margins may have limited scope of further expansion from
historically elevated levels. Together, based on our analysis, these factors point toward slower
but still healthy economic growth around 2.3% ahead, according to BofA Global Research.

• According to the BLS, “core” CPI inflation remained faster than desired in October, with
evidence of some broadening. The retreat in Brent oil from roughly $135 to $95 per barrel in
recent weeks has been encouraging, though elevated diesel prices and freight costs are still
spilling into other prices. Also, shelter seems to have run its disinflation course, accounting
for about a third of the October “core” CPI increase as excess rental inventory got absorbed.

Housing-services inflation is now close to its prepandemic average, providing a fading
disinflation tailwind.
All in all, growth is likely to moderate ahead, the tariff effect on inflation is dissipating,
and lower diesel prices would materially relieve near-term inflation pressure. Yet a set
of more structural supply constraints and potential inflation pressures remains to be
addressed. Rising electricity costs, infrastructure bottlenecks, skilled-labor shortages,

an aging population, housing costs and supply limitations, a wealthy segment of the
population relatively insensitive to inflation, disruptive trade and industrial policies, and
the resource demands associated with the AI-investment boom suggest a multitude of
potential inflation pressures if accommodated by persistent large fiscal deficits, excess
credit growth, a wage-cost spiral or unmitigated wealth effects. This strengthens the
case for measures to boost productive capacity and policies to better align nominal

spending with potential supply, if inflation is to sustainably return to target through
increased supply rather than higher reliance on Fed-induced demand suppression.
Restrictive interest rates may in fact delay the very supply response needed to alleviate
some of these inflation pressures.
Exhibit 1: Economy Rebounding From Tariff Shock But Growth Likely To Remain Moderate, In Line With Labor Supply And
Robust Productivity Underpinnings.
A) Growth led by manufacturing revival but surveys overall still consistent with moderate

economic conditions.
B) Easing economic policy uncertainty from its April 2025 tariff-related peak has renewed
business demand for labor, and labor supply responded in kind.

4 of 8 November 16, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
From Earnings Strength to Earnings Durability
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
U.S. Equities have faced a series of crosscurrents in recent months. At the index level, stocks
have seemingly gone nowhere. The S&P 500 has yet to break out of the sideways trading
range that began in early June, rising less than 1% over that period. But that relatively flat

performance masks considerable movement beneath the surface. As enthusiasm around the
AI trade dominated through the summer, market leadership narrowed and breadth steadily
deteriorated. By the end of September, nearly 60% of S&P 500 constituents were more than
15% below their 52-week closing highs, suggesting a sizable correction was already unfolding
beneath the headline index. October and November brought another shift in leadership as
intensifying headwinds around the AI trade pressured mega-cap Technology stocks, while

previously lagging areas began to recover. The Philadelphia Semiconductor Index fell as much
as 38% from its mid-June peak through early November and remains roughly 31% below that
high despite a recent partial recovery.
Against that volatile market backdrop, corporate fundamentals have told a much steadier
story. With roughly 91% of S&P 500 companies having reported as of November 13, Q3
earnings-per-share (EPS) growth is tracking around 32% YoY according to FactSet, which,

if sustained, would mark the third consecutive quarter of earnings growth above 25% and
the eighth consecutive quarter of double-digit growth. The magnitude of earnings growth in
recent quarters is unusual this far into an economic expansion and more closely resembles
the earnings rebounds typically seen following recessions. In fact, outside of the recoveries
following the 2000 Dot-com bubble peak, 2008/2009 Global Financial Crisis and 2020
pandemic, Q2 marked the fastest pace of earnings growth across the last three decades

(Exhibit 2). The underlying results have also remained strong, with revenue growth firmly in
double-digit territory and the Q3 net profit margin tracking its second-highest level since
FactSet began measuring the metric in 2009.
Exhibit 2: Earnings Growth Resembles a Post-Recession Rebound.

Yet equity markets have shown surprisingly little response to that earnings strength. According to

BofA Global Research, companies beating expectations on both earnings and sales outperformed
the S&P 500 by just 90 basis points the following day, compared with a historical average of 1.4
percentage points. Companies missing on both have traded roughly in line with the market, versus
an average historical underperformance of 2.5 percentage points. In turn, the resulting performance
gap between beats and misses is the narrowest since Q4 2000 and Q4 2001, following the March

2000 peak in the Dot-com bubble.1 The disconnect seems to be less about the strength of current
results than the increasingly high bar investors have set for what comes next.
Some moderation from here is almost inevitable. An increasingly difficult YoY comparison
base sets a high hurdle after several quarters of exceptional growth. BofA Global Research
expects S&P 500 EPS growth to slow from a projected 33% in 2026 to 12% in 2027, while
1 BofA Global Research, “Earnings Tracker: Week 4: 2021 beat rate, 2001 price action,” November 6, 2026.

Investment Implications
We continue to favor staying
invested while using periods of
market weakness to rebalance and
broaden portfolio exposure. As
earnings growth moderates and
market leadership becomes more
differentiated, investors should
remain highly diversified and
emphasize higher-quality exposure.

### Page 5

5 of 8 November 16, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
bottom-up consensus remains somewhat more optimistic at 16%. That would represent a
meaningful deceleration, but it would not necessarily signal deterioration as double-digit
earnings growth would still leave the fundamental backdrop on solid footing.
The bigger question for investors though is what could turn that expected earnings growth
normalization into a sharper slowdown. Midterm election results have introduced a new source

of uncertainty about the AI investment cycle, as voters and newly elected Democratic governors
in several states at the center of the data center boom have supported greater restrictions on
development. That risk matters disproportionately for the earnings outlook, with Technology
and Energy accounting for roughly 75% of Q3 S&P 500 earnings growth (Exhibit 3A). BofA
Global Research estimates that a hypothetical 20% reduction in 2027 AI capital spending

could translate into 35% to 40% downside to AI semiconductor EPS and lower S&P 500 EPS
by roughly 7% to 8%, before accounting for secondary effects. So far, however, a slowdown
has yet to emerge in company guidance, with hyperscalers continuing to raise capital spending
plans. While recent market repricing has reflected some of that uncertainty, the durability of AI
investment and earnings remains an important factor in the broader earnings outlook.

For now, the conditions that typically accompany a broader earnings downturn remain largely
absent. Economic activity continues to expand at an above-trend pace, with the Atlanta Fed
GDPNow model tracking 3.5% annualized real gross domestic product growth for Q4. Consumer
spending also remains firm heading into the holiday season. Bank of America aggregated credit
and debit card spending per household rose 5.8% YoY in October, or 4.6% excluding gasoline.2

Although both measures moderated from September, they remain close to the strong growth
rates recorded since the spring. That demand resilience continues to show through corporate
earnings results. According to FactSet, 84% of S&P 500 companies have reported actual EPS
above mean estimates, well above the five-year average of 78%. And that strength extends well
beyond the largest contributors to index earnings growth, with nine of the 11 S&P 500 sectors

seeing 80% or more of reporting companies exceed consensus EPS estimates (Exhibit 3B) and
all sectors tracking positive earnings growth for 2026. While the earnings cycle may be moving
beyond its strongest phase, the broader backdrop does not yet point to a fundamental downturn.
For investors, a moderating earnings backdrop should place greater emphasis on where
earnings growth is coming from and how durable that growth ultimately proves. Greater

market dispersion should favor companies with healthy operating cash flows, strong balance
sheets and the ability to translate investment into sustainable returns. That applies particularly
to the AI buildout. A slower or more disciplined pace of capital spending would not necessarily
signal an end to the investment cycle but could help separate the companies and projects that
can ultimately deliver on their investment from those where expectations have moved too

far, too fast. That type of differentiation should become increasingly important as the market
moves into a more measured phase of the cycle.

THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Mercantilist China Powers On: The Relentless Rise of Its Trade Surplus
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
And the boom continues. If investors have learned anything this year, it is that China’s not
ready to adjust its mercantilist growth model. Even in the face of higher U.S. tariffs and a
corresponding decline in exports to the U.S., China recorded a staggering $1T merchandise

trade surplus in just the first 10 months of this year. By the end of 2026, the country’s
trade surplus is on track to surpass 2025’s record and reach something closer to $1.3T3—or
triple the level at the start of the decade ($421 billion4) (Exhibit 4A).
Who is on the receiving end of all these exports? Hint: not the U.S., whose tariffs
taught the rest of the world that it can no longer count on America to absorb a significant
share of China’s exports. Per Exhibit 4B, as recently as 2019, the U.S. accounted for 20% of

exports from China. Today, that share is 10%, down sharply from 15% pre-Liberation Day.
That’s been a bitter pill to swallow for trading partners like the EU and Association of
Southeast Asian Nations (ASEAN) countries, whose year-to-date deficits with China have
ballooned by 29% and 34% YoY, respectively. Germany, whose local manufacturing industry
continues to struggle under the weight of competition from the mainland, has seen its
deficit with China double in just the last year. It’s no surprise, then, that the chorus of

complaints against China’s mercantilist approach continues to swell beyond Washington.
From quotas on electric vehicle imports in the EU to anti-dumping investigations in India to
product-specific tariffs in Mexico, Brazil, and South Africa, the world is pushing back against
China and its well-subsidized industries.
Looking ahead to 2027, a full-blown tit-for-tat global trade war is far from our base
case. However, we wouldn’t be surprised to see more targeted tariffs, investigations, and

industrial subsidies from developed and emerging markets alike, especially in strategic
sectors like autos/parts, chemicals, steel and basic metals, and other manufactured goods.
In the U.S., despite the Democratic sweep at the midterm elections, tariffs are likely here to
stay via legal measures like Section 232 and Section 301.
For China, more pushback from the rest of the world could force the economy to finally
come to terms with persistently depressed domestic demand. Meanwhile, investment

in domestic manufacturing should continue in the U.S. and Europe. We favor the former
over the latter in portfolios because the diversified nature of the U.S. economy, existing
protectionist measures, and tilt toward services and innovation leave growth relatively less
exposed to competition in manufacturing.
Exhibit 4: Taking Stock of China’s #1 Growth Engine.

Investment Implications
With China’s growth model still favoring
exports over domestic consumption,
resource protectionism and an
emphasis on domestic investment not
just in the U.S. but around the globe are

likely to continue to be themes in 2027.
We remain overweight the U.S., as its
well-diversified services-led economy
should be relatively less exposed to
China’s manufactured goods exports.
Still, the impact of competition from
China on U.S. technology supremacy is
an ongoing key risk to watch.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Chicago Fed National Activity Index is a monthly index designed to gauge overall economic activity and related inflationary pressure.
Consumer Price Index is a measure of the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a market basket of consumer goods and services. Indexes are available for the
U.S. and various geographic areas.
National Federation of Independent Business Small Business Optimism Index is a monthly composite economic indicator that measures the health, confidence, and expectations of small

investment advisory services, including the type of advice and assistance provided, the fees charged, and the rights and obligations of the parties. It is important to understand the differences,
particularly when determining which service or services to select. For more information about these services and their differences, speak with your Merrill financial advisor.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

## Document 353: Lean社区通过“形式化验证”：相信Anthropic数学家对黎曼猜想的证明是正确的

Source PDF: all report pdf/Lean社区通过“形式化验证”：相信Anthropic数学家对黎曼猜想的证明是正确的.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-22
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

传统数学突破靠同⾏逐⼀审阅论⽂、判断每⼀步推理是否成⽴。但⾯对超过 25 万⻚、短期
内⽆⼈能通读的证明草稿，形式化验证软件 Lean 成了数学界最快的 “ 公证处 ” 。
摘要：
11 ⽉ 21 ⽇，开源形式化验证社区 Lean 与 Mathlib 发布联合声明，确认 Anthropic 数学家 Levent
Alpöge 对黎曼猜想的证明成⽴。两家机构联合背书称：
11 ⽉ 20 ⽇，即 Anthropic 上市次⽇， Alpöge 宣布与 AI 模型 Claude 合作找到了黎曼猜想及⼴义黎曼
猜想的证明⽅法，并公开逾 1700 万⾏ Lean 源码。因超过 25 万⻚的⽂字版证明难以阅读⽽未公
开，加之宣布时点紧贴 IPO ，外界⼀度对其真伪与商业动机存疑，预测平台上的相关赔率也反映
出市场分歧。
24 ⼩时后， Lean 社区⽤四重形式化核验给出了计算机级别的回应。但机器验证与⼈类读懂证明
是两码事 —— 验证链上甚⾄出现了 Claude 编写的内核，部分收尾测试尚未结束，要想克雷数学

研究所的 100 万美元⼤奖也还需要经过⾄少两年的公开检验。
24 ⼩时核完 1700 万⾏代码： Lean 如何验真，边界⼜在哪？
传统数学突破靠同⾏逐⼀审阅论⽂、判断每⼀步推理是否成⽴。但⾯对超过 25 万⻚、短期内⽆
⼈能通读的证明草稿，形式化验证软件 Lean 成了数学界最快的 “ 公证处 ” 。
Lean 由巴西计算机科学家 Leonardo de Moura 于 2013 年创⽴，最初在微软研究院开发，后在亚
⻢逊云科技 AWS 延续推进， 2021 年推出重写版 Lean 4 ，现由 Lean FRO ⽀持。在这种编程语⾔
和证明助⼿中，数学家须把每个定义、前置结论和推理步骤写成计算机可读代码，由程序逐⼀
校验逻辑是否严密。
“ 完全有信⼼ Alpöge 对黎曼猜想的证明是正确的 ” 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

其始于 2017 年的社区共享数学库 Mathlib 规模已超 100 万⾏，⽂档中甚⾄给黎曼猜想留了⼀句注
脚，称构造其证明 “ 值⼀百万美元 ” 。 Lean 曾于 2021 年验证 Peter Scholze 的凝聚数学证明， 2023
年协助陶哲轩形式化多项式 Freiman-Ruzsa 猜想证明，也是 Google DeepMind 打造 AlphaProof
的底层⼯具，并被⽤于亚⻢逊 Cedar 策略语⾔及 Rust 代码验证。
但代码能跑通编译，并不等于黎曼猜想已被攻克。数学家 Kit Yates 在 Substack 专栏中指出，
Lean 校验虽远⽐让另⼀个 AI 评判严谨，专家仍须额外确认三点：代码证明的确实是黎曼猜想⽽
⾮更弱命题、定义忠实还原了 ζ 函数等标准对象、推理中未夹带额外假设。 Lean 语⾔参考⼿册也
警⽰，系统⽆法检查新公理是否⾃洽，更须警惕能 “ 证明⼀切 ” 的占位符sorry。
此次 Lean 社区敢于在⼀天内拍板，是因为 Alpöge 的代码库全部通过了伦敦帝国理⼯学院数学家

Kevin Buzzard 在 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇提出的四项硬标准。
第 ⼀ 关 是 核 对 “ 题 ⼲ ” 。 该 代 码 库 仅 直 接 依 赖 M a t h l i b ， 所 证 定 理 指 向 M a t h l i b 内 置 的
RiemannHypothesis标准定义 —— 在所有使 ζ 函数为零的复数中，除 -2 、 -4 、 -6 等平凡零点
外，实部均为 1/2 。该定义早已经过 Lean 和数论专家反复核验。
第⼆关是独⽴复现。这份拆为 14 个压缩⽂件、涵盖 175,187 个模块的代码库对算⼒要求极⾼，单
⼈完整验证约需 100GB 内存与 70GB 硬盘。⿇省理⼯学院数学家 Andrew Sutherland 使⽤ Lean 与
Mathlib v4.33.1 独⽴完成⽆错编译，且 Mathlib ⽂件与官⽅缓存⼀致。联合声明显⽰，已有数⼗
⼈复现编译，并⽣成完全⼀致的原始证明导出⽂件，体量达 61.6GB 。
第三关有关 “ 暗箱操作 ” 。社区另建仅导⼊ Mathlib 的隔离⽂件，动⽤comparator与con-leche

--compare两套⼯具⽐对，双双确认 Alpöge 所证命题与 Mathlib 原定义完全吻合。在公理审查
上， Sutherland 对主⽂件Main.lean中的五个定理运⾏#print axioms指令，结果显⽰它们
仅 依 赖 Lean 七 条 标 准 公 理 中 的 三 条 核 ⼼ 公 理 ——propext 、Classical.choice 与
Quot.sound ， 不 含 任 何sorryAx ， 且 全 部 源 ⾃ 核 ⼼ 库 。 “ 这 ⾥ ⾯ 有 猫 腻 的 概 率 近 似 为
零， ”Sutherland 对《科学美国⼈》说。
最后⼀关针对验证软件本⾝。 Lean 官⽅内核历史上曾出现可靠性漏洞，发现后 4 ⼩时即被修复，
数学家 Elliot Glazer 也将 Lean ⾃⾝存在漏洞的概率估计在 1% 以下。为隔绝源码潜在恶意代码、
规避单⼀内核缺陷，社区跳过源码，直接将 61.6GB 的惰性导出⽂件交由多语⾔独⽴内核交叉检
验。
除 Lean 官⽅内核及全量放⾏ 175,187 个模块的leanchecker、lean4lean外， Rust 编写的第

三⽅内核nanoda耗时 27 分 42 秒接受完整导出；con-leche仅⽤ 3 分 15 秒审完 146.8 万条声明，
⽆⼀拒绝；con-ron同样过关。con-leche与con-ron本⾝还附带相对于集合论的形式化⾃洽
证明，从数学逻辑上确保其⽆法接受错误证明。
验证通过的效⼒已体现在具体数学推论上。结合已有形式化成果， Sutherland ⻚⾯确认已从该证
明中推导出关于整数因数之和的 Robin 不等式与 Lagarias 不等式，同样只依赖三条标准公理。
1984 年，法国数学家 Guy Robin 证明，对任何⼤于 5040 的整数 n ，其所有因数之和⼩于  这⼀直
观命题与黎曼猜想完全等价 —— 其中 γ 为欧拉常数， e^γ 约等于 1.78 。
不过，对照 Lean 联合声明与 Sutherland 的 MIT 验证⻚，这场验证仍有⼏处尚未彻底收⼝的技术边
界。
Lean 联合声明虽宣布comparator和官⽅内核均已通过，但未公布具体⼯具版本与详细输出⽇

志。截⾄ 11 ⽉ 21 ⽇ 12:55 UTC ， Sutherland 本⼈在 Lean v4.36.0 上的端到端comparator运⾏已
完成命题⽐对与公理检查，内核阶段仍在继续， Lean 默认与严格内核也尚未出结果；他此前的

### Page 3

构建与导出未在沙盒中完成，尚待与沙盒构建结果⽐对。⾄于 Lean v4.36.0 捆绑的旧版con-
leche和con-ron因不⽀持证明中的⼀种类型返回退出码 2 ， Sutherland ⻚⾯注明这并不意味着
证明被拒绝。
在证明范围上也存在⼝径差。 Elliot Glazer 于 11 ⽉ 20 ⽇称形式化命题实为⼴义黎曼猜想，
Sutherland ⻚⾯则更为审慎：主定理是 Mathlib 定义的黎曼猜想，其余四个定理针对狄利克雷 L 函
数，⻚⾯仅在此限定意义上提及 “GRH” 。 Sutherland 同时提醒，即便公理检查⽆误，最终结论仍
以 Mathlib 定义忠于数学本意、验证内核⾃⾝可靠为前提。
从证明到 “ 判卷 ”
把视⻆拉向幕后， Claude 的⾝影贯穿了从提出证明到参与 “ 判卷 ” 的多个环节。
按 Alpöge 附上的研究时间线，这项⼯作始于 10 ⽉ 6 ⽇的 “Stepanov ⾏列式 ” ，⾄当地时间 11 ⽉ 15 ⽇
深夜⾄ 16 ⽇凌晨告成， 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇的记录上仅剩 “GRH” 三个字⺟。该时间线据称由 Claude 整理。

Alpöge 表⽰，⾃⼰负责指明⽅向， Claude 随后提出突破性步骤 —— ⼀条⻓度约等于所界定素数
和平⽅根的 “theta 提升 ” 链，每次提升对应经典函数域论证所依赖、在整数域上却极难找到的 “ 重
数 ” 。 “ 在接近终点时， Claude 远远超过了我。不， Claude 简直是在终点附近⻜翔、跳舞、闪耀， ”
他在声明中写道。
到了核验阶段， Claude 依然在场。 Sutherland 验证⻚开头署名显⽰，整套检查通过 Claude
Code 运⾏；⽽ 3 分 15 秒审完 146.8 万条声明的con-leche，据 Lean 创始⼈ Leonardo de Moura
上⽉在博客中介绍，是⾸个⽤ Lean 写成并被证明⾃洽的内核 —— 代码与⾃洽证明均由 Claude 完
成，由 Lean FRO 的 Joachim Breitner 严格把关。因为有形式化⾃洽证明兜底，且最终结论与
Rust 内核nanoda等多个第三⽅独⽴内核⼀致，验证信⽤并不取决于单⼀⼯具。

这种推进速度超出了 Anthropic 三个⽉前的⾃⾝预期。今年 8 ⽉，公司曾公布未公开版 Claude 配
合 Lean 的研究成果，将关键线上零点⽐例下界从 41.6% 推⾼⾄ 67.2% ，并通过了comparator
检验。当时 Anthropic 明确表态，并不预期这些技术能最终证明黎曼猜想。
仅仅三个⽉后就实现跨越，却恰好撞上 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇ IPO 后的敏感窗⼝。 Alpöge 在声明中主动承认
“ 精确的时机⾮常糟糕 ” ，透露完成后曾询问 Anthropic ⾸席执⾏官 Dario Amodei 能否公开， “ 最终
我们弄清楚了，确实存在需要遵守的限制 ” 。他否认外界对发布时机的负⾯猜测，称这种 “ 明显的
愤世嫉俗的看法 ” 是错误的，并坦⾔⾃⼰ “ 实在⽆法再等下去了 ” ，愿承担全部责任。
舆论狂欢与学界克制
北京时间 11 ⽉ 21 ⽇ 23:12 ， Lean 官⽅公告在社交平台 X 发布，迅速激起科技圈与主流学界的讨
论。
AI 圈情绪迅速沸腾： AI 研究者姚顺⾬早在 20 ⽇晚便发帖惊呼 “OMG, Riemann Hypothsis?!” ；官

⽅声明出炉后， AI 资讯作者 Rohan Paul 概括称 “167 年后，黎曼猜想终于被 AI 完成了 ” ，评论账号
Dr Singularity 则感叹 “What a time to be alive” 。⽹友还翻出 OpenAI ⾸席执⾏官 Sam Altman 于
2022 年在 X 上半开玩笑追问 “ 黎曼猜想什么时候能搞定 ” 的旧帖，调侃 “Anthropic 抢在你前⾯做到
了 ” 。

### Page 4

主流学者的措辞则明显更紧。 11 ⽉ 21 ⽇早间，在 Lean 官⽅联合声明尚未发布前，哥伦⽐亚⼤学
数学与物理教授 Brian Greene 发帖介绍 AI“ 可能攻克了凌驾于所有难题之上的黎曼猜想 ” ，但⽤词
保留余地，并⾮其本⼈独⽴核验。罗格斯⼤学数学系主任 Alex Kontorovich 虽对证明成功愈发乐
观，当⽇在 X 上的动作也仅是转发《 Quanta Magazine 》关于 Alpöge 声明的报道。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

3 条评论
在功劳归属上，学界同样拒绝将此简化为 “AI 独⼒破题 ” 。普林斯顿⼤学⼀位数学与 AI 研究⼈员强
调，⼈类参与仍是利⽤⼤语⾔模型解决数学问题的最佳⽅式， Alpöge 应当获得与 Claude 同等的
认可。 Sutherland 在独⽴编译通过后，也称这是⼀场 “ ⼈类的胜利 ” 。
更棘⼿的现实在于，机器已经验证，⼈类却还读不懂。曼彻斯特⼤学数学家 Thomas Bloom 在 X
上直⾔，他分不清更令⾃⼰震惊的是 AI 声称证明了黎曼猜想，还是 AI ⽣成了⼀份转化为⼈类可读
⽂本后超过 25 万⻚、且逻辑连贯的证明。
2006 年菲尔兹奖得主陶哲轩在个⼈博客中给出初步判断：这份证明似乎综合运⽤了朗兰兹纲领
和算术⼏何的现代研究成果，验证与消化仍需时间， “ 好在这⼀次，⾄少还有⼀位⼈类作者可以

沟通 ” 。
接下来，拆解与简化这份庞⼤证明将成为数学界的核⼼课题。 Lean 与 Mathlib 社区在声明末尾表
⽰，期待数学界接下来的⼯作能够阐释该证明中的新思想。 Alpöge 本⼈也预计，这份体量惊⼈
的证明 “ 很可能会被⼤幅简化，⽽且可能很快就能实现 ” 。
即便计算机已经给出了肯定答复，数学界的正式加冕仍须跨越漫⻓的制度⻔槛。按克雷数学研
究所规则，千禧年⼤奖难题的 100 万美元奖⾦要求解答必须正式发表、经过⾄少两年的公开检
验，并获得国际数学界普遍认可。
对 Alpöge ⽽⾔，他⼝中这个 “ 科学领域某种更伟⼤事物的⼀个黎明 ” ，距离真正尘埃落定还有数年
时间。

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH 
EARNINGS AGGREGATES 
STOXX 600 Earnings Outlook provides analysis and commentary on aggregate earnings estimate revisions, growth rates and valuations.

Analyst: Tajinder Dhillon, CFA                                         November 5, 2026 
tajinder.dhillon@lseg.com

STOXX 600 EARNINGS OUTLOOK  
EUROPEAN AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS

 Third quarter earnings are expected to increase 25.0% from Q3 2026. Excluding the Energy sector, earnings 
are expected to increase 9.5%.

 Third quarter revenue is expected to increase 11.9% from Q3 2026. Excluding the Energy sector, revenues are 
expected to increase 6.9%.

 212 companies in the STOXX 600 have reported earnings to date for Q3 2026. Of these, 58.0% reported results 
exceeding analyst estimates.

 257 companies in the STOXX 600 have reported revenue to date for Q3 2026. Of these, 64.2% reported revenue 
exceeding analyst estimates.

 During the week of November 9, 40 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

Through November 5, 212 companies in the STOXX 600 Index reported earnings for Q3 2026. Of these, 58% reported 
earnings above analyst estimates. In a typical quarter (sin ce 2012), 54% of companies beat estimates and 39% miss 
estimates.

In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.2% above estimates, which is below the 5.9% long-term 
(since 2012) average surprise factor.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the STOXX 600 fo r Q3 2026 is 25%. The STOXX 600 expects to see share-
weighted earnings of €164.4B in Q3 2026, compared to shar e-weighted earnings of €131.6B (based on the year-ago 
earnings of the current constituents) in Q3 2025.

Eight of the ten sectors in the index expect to see an improv ement in earnings relative to Q3 2025. The Energy sector 
has the highest earnings growth rate for the quarter, wh ile the Real Estate has the weakest anticipated growth 
compared to Q3 2025.

The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (153 .4%) of any sector. It is expected to earn €35.7B in Q3 
2026, compared to earnings of €14.1B in Q3 2025.

The Real Estate sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (-69.6%) of any sector. It is expected to earn €0.9B in Q3 
2026, compared to earnings of €3.1B in Q3 2025.

The Consumer Cyclicals sector has the lowest revenue growth rate (-1.2%) of any sector. It is expected to generate 
sales of €196.8B in Q3 2026, compared to sales of €199.1B in Q3 2025.

## Document 375: Sovereign debt_ When Short-Term Shocks and Shifts in Debt Structure Leave a Lasting Fiscal Burden — The Case of Brazil (2026-11-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sovereign debt_ When Short-Term Shocks and Shifts in Debt Structure Leave a Lasting Fiscal Burden — The Case of Brazil (2026-11-12).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

10 November 2026
The bank
for a changing

SOVEREIGN DEBT: WHEN SHORT-TERM SHOCKS AND SHIFTS IN DEBT STRUCTURE LEAVE A LASTING FISCAL
BURDEN — THE CASE OF BRAZIL
Salim Hammad
Brazil’s recent post-election yield shock risks leaving a lasting imprint on public finances — compounded by already high interest rates and a
changing debt structure. Large refinancing needs and a rising share of floating-rate debt will transmit rapidly and significantly higher interest

rates to the debt service through distinct but reinforcing channels.
SOURCES : MACROBOND, BCB
Brazil’s presidential elections triggered a sharp repricing of Brazil’s rate outlook

CHART 1
An abrupt post-election market awakening

In the wake of the 2 nd round of the general elections in late October, Brazilian markets — which had expected a Flavio Bolsonaro
victory — sold off sharply after Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva won a fourth presidential term. Both the equity and FX markets
experienced large intraday drops (14% of the Ibovespa stock index and -11% BRL vs. USD). Meanwhile, the yield on Brazil’s 10-year
government bond rose 250 basis points to 15.5%, reaching its highest level in over a decade (Chart 1) as investors worried about

the fiscal impact of costly campaign promises amidst large and persistent fiscal imbalances.
Fiscal risks have reshaped Brazil’s public debt structure, magnifying the cost of persistently high interest rates
Absent a confidence-infusing fiscal adjustment, Brazil’s interest bill is unlikely to decline in the short term, maintaining upward
pressure on public debt (83% of GDP in October 2026) and limiting budgetary leeway.
Three transmission channels, amplified by changing features of the debt structure, explain this persistence.

The first channel: The rise in long-term yields will increase rollover costs.
The sovereign must refinance roughly 20% of its debt stock each year. An average maturity of 4.1 years spreads refinancing over
time but does not shield the interest bill from a sustained rise in borrowing costs. As maturing securities are replaced, higher
issuance rates progressively lift the average borrowing cost — even for fixed-rate debt, whose interest costs remain unchanged

until refinancing. A sustained yield shock therefore feeds into debt servicing as the stock rolls over.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 10 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The second channel: Demand for floating-rate instruments will strengthen until confidence in fiscal policy improves.
In turn, a larger share of SELIC-linked debt heightens the debt stock’s sensitivity to the prevailing high policy rate. Already SELIC-linked
securities reached a record 52.6% of the debt stock in August 2026, around 12 percentage points above their share at the start of President

Lula’s last term, versus a low of 18.4% in September 2014 (Chart 2). Weaker demand for fixed-rate securities with maturities exceeding five
years, amid increased fiscal uncertainty, has encouraged this shift 1.
The third channel: The BCB may delay rate cuts until fiscal policy over the next mandate is better defined.
A slower easing cycle would prolong high interest costs on SELIC-linked debt, whose servicing costs adjust with the policy rate without

waiting for refinancing. With these securities accounting for a growing share of the debt stock, delayed monetary easing would postpone
relief for the Treasury’s interest bill — even if long-term market yields subsequently ease.
These channels operate at different speeds: a rise in long-term yields increases rollover costs, whereas floating-rate debt reprices with
the SELIC rate, not automatically with 10-year yields. Meanwhile, a slower monetary easing cycle and stronger demand for floating-rate

securities compound pressure on the interest bill.
The fiscal–monetary policy nexus is therefore likely to remain a source of strain on Brazil’s public finances: fiscal uncertainty could delay
monetary easing, while greater floating-rate exposure would prolong the burden of high policy rates on the interest bill.
salim.hammad@bnpparibas.com
1 Fiscal slippage and uncertainty make investors less willing to lock in nominal returns over long horizons: if inflation or market yields rise, the purchasing power of fixed payments

declines or the bonds’ market value falls. Investors therefore demand higher yields to bear this risk, making long-term fixed-rate funding more expensive for the Treasury. By issuing
floating-rate bonds instead, the Treasury offers returns that adjust with the SELIC rate, sustaining demand, securing financing and reducing the risk of failed auctions. However
longer floating-rate maturities may reduce rollover risk, but do not remove the repricing exposure. Floating-rate issuance helps secure financing but transfers interest-rate risk to

the Treasury.
SOURCES : NATIONAL TREASURY, BNP PARIBAS

The scenario, forecasts and nowcasts of the Economic Research 
–

### Page 2

UNITED STATES 
The US economy is expected to grow above its potential in 2026, with an average annual growth rate of 2.2%, close to the 2025 rate (2.1%). 
This apparent resilience to the uncertainty, tariff and energy shocks, together with above-trend productivity growth, masks K-shaped growth, 
driven by investment linked to AI-optimism and consumption by the wealthiest amid a wealth effect (historically high stock market valuations). 
Inflation overshooting is set to continue (3.5% in 2026) at least through 2028, largely because of the rise in oil prices and tariffs – although the 
impact of these latter appears to be less significant than expected. The labour market is showing clear signs of improvement that should bring 
the unemployment rate down toward 4.0%. Given this shift in price and employment risks amid dynamic growth, the FOMC proceeded with a 
first rate hike at September’s meeting and we expect two further 25bp increases in December 2026 and January 2027, bringing the Fed Funds 
target range at 4.25% - 4.50%.

CHINA 
Economic growth rebounded to 4.5% y/y in Q3 2026 (1.2% q/q) after 4.3% y/y in Q2 and 5.0% in Q1. It is projected to reach 4.6% in 2026 as a 
whole, down from 5% in 2025. Growth remains characterised by a K-shaped trajectory, and the gap between the strong performance of exports 
and the fragility of sectors that rely on domestic demand continues to widen. Retail sales barely increased in the first ten months of 2026, the 
crisis in the property sector continues and household confidence remains low. Yet new fiscal policy measures could help support investment 
and private consumption in the very short term. Deflationary pressures are persisting, given weakness in domestic demand. Energy prices and 
electronic goods prices are increasing, producer price inflation is accelerating (5.7% y/y in October), but consumer price inflation remains low 
(0.7% y/y).

EUROZONE 
The economy has shown resilience in the face of the energy shock: GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3, and the 1.0% growth carry-over for 2026 
supports our forecast of 1.1% for the year as a whole (compared to 1.3% in 2025) with the risk tilted to the upside. Growth is expected to 
strengthen to 1.6% in 2027, bolstered by increased spending on defence and infrastructure, as well as the global investment cycle in AI. Annual 
average inflation is thus expected to reach 3.1% in 2026 (from 2.1% in 2025), and 3.2% in 2027. Given persistent inflation and resilient growth, 
we anticipate another 25bp hike from the ECB in December 2026, bringing the deposit rate to 2.75%, followed by an extended pause. That 
said, stronger than expected second-round effects could lead to more tightening than our current baseline.

Contacts

stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

### Page 3

FRANCE 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 2026 (following stagnation in Q2), weighed down by a decline in aeronautics production (delay in the 
delivery of inputs) but supported by service sector output and purchases of electric vehicles. Overall, growth is expected to slow to 0.3% in 
2026 (after 0.9% in 2025) before rebounding to 0.8% in 2027. In parallel, inflation should reach 2.5% in 2026 (compared with 0.9% in 2025), 
before edging down slightly in 2027 (2.4%).

UNITED KINGDOM 
UK GDP growth would accelerate to 1.6% in 2026 (1.2% in 2025). The economy has so far weathered both the energy price shock and domestic 
political volatility, boosted by domestic demand, notably investment. After a series of strong prints from Q1 to Q3, real GDP growth is expected 
to decelerate in the last quarter of the year, as higher energy prices should feed inflation. It should then slow to 1.3% in 2027 on the back of 
higher inflation and more restrictive monetary conditions. Inflation should climb to 3.9% in Q4 2026, peaking in Q1 2027 (at 4.5%) before 
gradually slowing. Still, it is set to remain stubbornly above target, leading to mounting second-round effects risks. In response, the BoE has 
finally raised its Bank Rate to 4.00% early November. If a second-rate hike looks likely in February, this is not, for now, our call and our baseline 
scenario projects that diminishing inflation will allow monetary easing to resume in the second half of 2027, with two cuts taking the Bank 
Rate down to 3.50% by year-end.

JAPAN 
Following 1.2% in 2025, Japan's GDP growth is expected to decelerate to 0.9% in 2026, before rebounding to 1.0% in 2027. Higher inflation 
and production costs associated with the energy shock are expected to weigh on the economy’s overall performance. On the other hand, the 
latter is supported by fiscal policy and AI-related investment. Inflation has generally overshot the 2% y/y target since 2022. Accordingly, the 
Bank of Japan initiated a cautious process of “adjustment in the degree of monetary accommodation” in 2024, lifting the policy rate to 1.25% 
so far (previously negative) – the highest since 1995. We expect two more hikes by the end of Q1 2027, followed by a return to a more cautious 
pace until the terminal rate of +2.5% is reached in H2 2028. Japan is facing long-term rates pressure, illustrated by historically high 10- and  
30-year yields, probably fueled by the level of public debt and the pace of monetary adjustment.

EXCHANGE RATES 
We expect Fed hikes to temporarily offset structural headwinds and support the dollar. EUR/USD would target 1.14 by end-2026, before the 
dollar's structural downtrend resumes toward 1.20 by end-2027, although political and fiscal risk may weigh on the euro next year. The USD/JPY 
appreciation would continue but at a more moderate pace, with the BoJ still behind the curve despite its hikes. By contrast, GBP/USD would 
see relative support underpinned by a modest fiscal risk premium under the new Labour government, and resilient growth.

## Document 398: Top of Mind - Higher Bond Yields_ How Concerning_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Top of Mind - Higher Bond Yields_ How Concerning_.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs (Top of Mind)
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 31
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Global bond yields have risen to multi-decade highs, yet growth and markets have
remained remarkably resilient. But with some pressures beginning to emerge (e.g. in
European sovereign bond markets), can this resilience last, or will more vulnerabilities
be revealed? We ask the IMF’s Tobias Adrian, Moody’s Analytics’ Mark Zandi, and GS
economists. Adrian is relatively sanguine about the economy’s resilience, though
notes downside risks, while GS economists expect only a modest growth drag from

higher rates, but a more consequential one if yields remain near current or higher
levels. Zandi is generally more concerned, describing the US economy as vulnerable
rather than resilient, and sees one-in-three odds of the economy going off the r ails.
Meanwhile, GS strategists and analysts are not especially concerned about banks or
markets more broadly, but warn that further increases in yields or rate volatility could
test risk assets. Key to watch: whether the increasingly interconnected and leveraged

AI investment boom can continue to withstand higher rates.
Tobias Adrian, Special Advisor and former Director of Monetary and Capital Markets, IMF
“The global economy has demonstrated considerable resilience in the face of multiple severe shocks, and I would not be
surprised if it continues to shrug off higher yields, pr ovided they do not keep rising sharply in a disorderly fashion. At the same
time, I do worry about downside risks.”
Mark Zandi, Chief Economist, Moody’s Analytics
